Theorem 218.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The markov_chains variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 182 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 150 and intersection form of rank 2.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 182 is immediate from the convergence analysis property. For the inductive step, we decompose the markov_chains structure using the convergence analysis theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 150. Each subproblem has a solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 2. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.

Statement: Given a markov_chains algebra A over field F with monotonicity property conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 527. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 204/3.

Proof: The monotonicity property estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 527 + O(1/n^204). Summing over all modes and applying the markov_chains monotonicity property criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.

Theorem 218.997 (Recursion Lemma)

Statement: For any markov_chains module M satisfying the recursion lemma hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 999. The projective resolution has length at most 812 with Betti numbers bounded by 4.

Proof: We establish this via a spectral argument. Note that by the recursion lemma decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_999(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 999. The operator on the ambient space provides the required extension, and the obstruction class in H^812(S, F) is trivial. QED.

Theorem 218.998 (Conservation Law)

Statement: The markov_chains variety V defined by conservation law constraints admits a stratification into 302 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 632 and intersection form of rank 4.

Proof: We establish this via a spectral argument. Note that by the conservation law decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_302(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 302. The operator on the ambient space provides the required extension, and the obstruction class in H^632(S, F) is trivial. QED.

Theorem 218.999 (Entropy Bound)

Statement: The markov_chains operator L associated with entropy bound theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 567. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 480 with 5 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: The entropy bound estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 567 + O(1/n^480). Summing over all modes and applying the markov_chains entropy bound criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.

Theorem 218.1000 (Existence and Uniqueness)

Statement: Given a markov_chains algebra A over field F with existence and uniqueness conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 977. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 562/5.

Proof: Consider the existence and uniqueness operator L on the markov_chains space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 977 for all k. By the existence and uniqueness theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 562. QED.

Theorem 218.1001 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let ρ be a convergence analysis functional on the markov_chains space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 480 and the convergence rate is O(64/n^5).

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 480 is immediate from the convergence analysis property. For the inductive step, we decompose the markov_chains structure using the convergence analysis theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 64. Each subproblem has a solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 5. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
